([okracomputer/skills](https://github.com/okracomputer/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/okrapdf) · 1 skill, 1 MCP config)
- **okrdev**: OKRs as markdown files in your own repo, with an AI coach that checks new work against your key results, pre-drafts weekly check-ins, and parks distractions. ([backedbydata-co/okrdev](https://github.com/backedbydata-co/okrdev) · ★1 · 8 skills)
- **okt-agents**: Five OKT research agents (orchestrator, research, investigation, synthesizer, super-synthesizer) plus the okt-setup skill for configuring the OKT MCP server. ([openktree/open-knowledge-tree](https://github.com/openktree/open-knowledge-tree/tree/HEAD/ai-plugins) · ★3 · 1 skill, 12 agents)
